## Cinematic New Year 2026 Birthday Photoshoot Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "prompt": "Cinematic nighttime New Year celebration birthday photoshoot at a beach or open outdoor location. A stylish young woman is sitting on the ground, smiling softly and looking directly at the camera, holding a party popper as colorful confetti explodes in the air. She is wearing a trendy red patterned shirt over a white top, paired with white pants and clean white sneakers. Soft teal and blue lighting creates a dreamy, festive atmosphere. In front of her is a beautifully decorated birthday cake with glowing candles. Beside the cake are large neon-style glowing numbers “{argument name="year" default="2026"}” in cyan blue, and a digital clock displaying “12:00 AM”. The background is dark with subtle bokeh lights, cinematic depth of field. Ultra-realistic photography, sharp facial details, natural skin texture, professional studio-grade lighting, high contrast, modern cinematic color grading, 4K resolution, Instagram-style aesthetic, joyful New Year celebration mood. Preserve exact face identity — do not change facial structure, features, skin tone, or expression.",
  "negative_prompt": "face change, different person, altered facial features, blur, low resolution, cartoon, anime, over-smoothing skin, extra limbs, distorted body, watermark, text artifacts, oversaturated colors",
  "style": "ultra-realistic cinematic photography",
  "lighting": "soft teal and blue cinematic lighting",
  "camera": "DSLR, shallow depth of field, bokeh background",
  "quality": "4K, high detail, professional",
  "aspect_ratio": "4:5"
}
```

## Detailed Cinematic Selfie Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "type": "photographic_reproduction",
  "style": "candid_indoor_selfie",
  "aspect_ratio": "3:4",
  "subject_anchor": {
    "primary_subject": "Young woman taking a selfie",

    "interaction": "Cheek-to-cheek contact, bodies pressed together, laughing expressions"
  },
  "pose_geometry": {

      "head_position": "Tilted approx 15 degrees right towards Santa",
      "arm_state": "Left arm extended forward towards camera lens (selfie arm), highly foreshortened, forearm visible as blur at bottom edge",
      "shoulder_state": "Shoulders angled, right shoulder pressed against Santa's chest/shoulder area",
      "spine": "Slight forward lean, compressed posture typical of selfie framing"
    },
    "subject_2_santa": {
      "head_position": "Tilted approx 20 degrees left towards woman",
      "arm_state": "Right arm wrapped around woman's far shoulder, hand visible wearing white glove resting on her upper arm",
      "body_orientation": "Turned slightly towards woman"
    }
  },
  "anatomy_and_details": {
    "facial_features_woman": {
      "expression": "Broad open-mouth smile, teeth visible, eyes slightly squinted from smiling",
      "skin": "Fair tone, natural texture, slight shine on forehead and cheeks, no airbrushing",
      "hair": "Brown, medium length, loose, falling behind shoulders, natural flyaways"
    },
    "facial_features_santa": {
      "expression": "Jovial open-mouth laugh, eyes crinkled behind glasses",
      "skin": "Ruddy/reddish complexion, deep wrinkles/crow's feet around eyes",
      "hair_beard": "Dense white synthetic-looking beard and mustache, frizzy texture",
      "accessories": "Wire-rimmed rectangular spectacles sitting low on nose"
    }
  },
  "clothing_and_fabric": {
    "woman_attire": {
      "top": "Green bikini top with intricate paisley/tribal pattern, triangle cut",
      "outerwear": "Grey/olive knitted cardigan, open front, coarse ribbed texture, slouching off shoulders",
      "bottoms": "Blue denim jeans, waistband visible"
    },
    "santa_attire": {
      "suit": "Red velvet material, slight sheen, heavy folds at elbow",
      "trim": "Thick white faux fur trim on coat opening and hat",
      "belt": "Wide black leather belt, large rectangular brass/gold buckle visible at bottom right",
      "hat": "Standard red Santa hat with white fur band"
    }
  },
  "environment_and_background": {

      "center_back": "Window with grid panes, dark outside",
      "right_back": "Christmas tree, out of focus, warm white string lights, ornaments (gold/red balls)",
      "walls": "Beige/cream painted walls, wall sconce light"
    }
  }
```

```
she is sitting next to two animated characters (a clever fox and a brave rabbit), friendly expressions, they are hugging each other warmly, natural interaction, realistic shadow blending between real person and animated characters, seamless composition, movie-quality render

camera style: Sony A7, 50mm lens, realistic skin texture, smooth color grading, crisp details, gentle bokeh

atmosphere: cozy, cinematic, heart-warming, winter vacation, polished professional photography

--ar 2:3 --v 6 --style cinematic --uplight"
```

## Disposable Camera Party Portrait Prompt

> Minimal, retro portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use this photo attached to create a disposable camera–inspired candid portrait in the style of Petra Collins.
Hair (LOCKED – must remain identical every time):
As the reference photo 
Expression & Face:
Playful, slightly pouty candid expression. Eyes glance upward with a mildly bored, unserious, spontaneous look—not posed. Lips softly pursed. Natural skin texture visible, minimal makeup with soft blush and natural lip tone, no heavy retouching.
Pose & Framing:
Casual upper-body snapshot framing. Subject slightly off-center, shoulders relaxed, body angled subtly. Arms rest naturally close to the torso. Feels like a quick party photo taken mid-moment.
Attire & Accessories (high detail):
Wearing a simple fitted black spaghetti-strap top, casual and understated. Fabric reflects the flash subtly with slight sheen. No statement jewelry—focus remains on hair, expression, and accessories.
Sunglasses (DETAILED – 2026):
Oversized black plastic party sunglasses resting on top of the head. The lenses feature bold rhinestone-style numbers spelling “{argument name="year" default="2026"}”, evenly spaced across both lenses. The numbers sparkle harshly under direct flash, with uneven reflections, tiny blown highlights, and visible plastic texture. Sunglasses sit slightly crooked, reinforcing the candid, unpolished party feel.
Lighting & Camera:
Shot using a cheap 35mm disposable film camera. Direct on-camera flash only—harsh, flat, frontal lighting. Overexposed highlights on skin, hair, sunglasses, and balloons. Deep shadows behind the subject. Visible grain, slight blur, imperfect focus, no digital sharpness.
Background & Balloons (highly detailed):
Metallic foil fringe curtain in the background with silver and pastel reflections. Large metallic number balloons float nearby, partially cropped by the frame. Balloons show realistic seams, wrinkles, and creases, reflecting the flash with distorted highlights. Thin curling balloon strings hang unevenly, adding clutter and realism.
Color & Texture:
Slight film color cast, saturated but imperfect tones. Whites slightly blown, blacks mildly crushed. Strong grain throughout, soft edges, subtle color inconsistencies typical of disposable film.
Overall Mood:
Intimate, nostalgic, playful, and unfiltered—feels like a real late-night party snapshot taken by a friend, not a styled shoot.
Style Keywords:
Petra Collins inspired, disposable camera flash, candid party portrait, 2026 sunglasses, metallic balloons, foil fringe backdrop, 35mm film grain, imperfect framing, nostalgic realism
```

```
A cozy, cinematic winter scene inside a glass cable car in the Swiss Alps. A young man wearing a matte black puffer jacket over a neutral sweater and dark jeans sits on a wooden bench, looking calmly at the camera with a relaxed, confident expression. He has neatly styled dark hair and a natural, realistic look. The puffer jacket has realistic quilting, soft fabric sheen, and subtle creases, adding warmth and modern winter style.

On his left, {argument name="character 1" default="Nick Wilde from Zootopia"}, the sly red fox, leans in casually, wearing his signature green shirt and striped tie with a confident smirk. On his right, {argument name="character 2" default="Judy Hopps from Zootopia"}, the cheerful gray rabbit police officer in her blue uniform, sits close with an energetic, friendly expression. The man has one arm comfortably around each character, creating a wholesome, cinematic bonding moment.

Outside the cable car’s panoramic glass windows, vast snow-covered Alpine mountains stretch into the distance beneath a clear blue sky. A red alpine train crosses a stone bridge in the background, adding depth and story to the scene. Bright natural daylight, crisp winter air, ultra-detailed textures. Photorealistic human seamlessly blended with high-quality animated characters, accurate lighting interaction, soft natural shadows, sharp focus, cozy luxury travel aesthetic. 4:5 aspect ratio, high resolution.
```

## Identity-Preserving Storybook Illustration

> Minimal, elle çizilmiş illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "prompt": "Use the uploaded image strictly as the identity and pose reference. Preserve the same person, facial structure, hairstyle, age, gender, body proportions, and overall likeness without adding or removing any person. Do not alter identity. Transform the subject into a hand-drawn storybook-style illustration created with colored pencils and soft watercolor washes.\
\
Illustration Style:\
Hand-drawn colored pencil illustration with clean, confident line art and slightly rough, organic pencil outlines. Pencil strokes should be clearly visible and layered, giving a natural hand-drawn feel. Combine this with gentle watercolor wash textures that softly blend colors without overpowering the pencil lines.\
\
Color & Texture:\
Use bright pastel colors with lighter, cheerful, and vivid tones while keeping natural saturation. Colors should feel soft and cozy, never harsh or neon. Apply flat yet detailed coloring with subtle watercolor gradients. Minimal shadows, soft highlights only, no dramatic lighting. No realism, no photo texture, no hyper-detail.\
\
Facial Features & Expression:\
Simplified, cute facial features in a semi-cartoon realistic proportion. Dot-style eyes, small gentle smiles, soft rounded face shapes. Expressions should feel warm, friendly, innocent, and approachable. Keep facial details minimal but expressive.\
\
Mood & Atmosphere:\
Warm, cozy, and cheerful atmosphere with a wholesome children’s book illustration feel. Friendly and comforting tone, suitable for a storybook or picture book. The overall mood should feel calm, joyful, and inviting.\
\
Rendering Rules:\
High clarity illustration, clean edges, no blur. No photographic realism, no cinematic lighting, no 3D render, no digital painting look. Avoid heavy shading, deep shadows, glossy skin, or realistic textures.\
\
Background:\
Simple, soft, illustrated background that complements the subject without distraction. Use pastel colors, gentle shapes, and minimal detail to maintain a storybook aesthetic.\
\
Final Output:\
A cozy, cheerful, hand-illustrated children’s book style artwork with colored pencil linework and soft watercolor textures, maintaining exact identity from the uploaded image while transforming it into a warm, friendly, semi-cartoon illustration.",
  "negative_prompt": "photorealism, hyper-realistic, realistic skin texture, photo texture, cinematic lighting, dramatic shadows, dark tones, neon colors, glossy skin, 3D render, oil painting, digital painting, anime style, manga style, vector art, pixel art, blur, noise, grain, extra people, face change, identity change, exaggerated realism",
  "quality": "high",
  "clarity": "very high",
  "style_strength": "strong illustration style",
  "reference_usage": "face and identity preservation required"
}
```

## Hyper-Realistic Miniature Scene in a WeChat Chat Bubble

> Fotogerçekçi logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Designated characters: Two real, living people scaled down to miniature size, see reference image

Text content: {argument name="text content" default="[Text content]"}

Timestamp: {argument name="timestamp" default="3:33 PM"}

Hyper-realistic overhead macro photography work. The main subject is a standard WeChat sender's light green chat bubble, which is used as a long dining table. [Designated characters] sit at both ends of the bubble.

Key character details: Emphasize that they are absolutely not plastic models; they have clearly visible skin pores, natural hair details, and extremely realistic fabric textures and wrinkles. They are enjoying miniature models of real food that looks steaming hot and freshly cooked, appropriate for their character identity, and looking up at the camera.

WeChat unique visual elements: The interior of the chat bubble only contains the text content: “{argument name="text content" default="[Text content]"}”. A gray WeChat-style timestamp: “{argument name="timestamp" default="[Timestamp]"}” is displayed centrally directly above the chat bubble.

Background details: The entire background is completely filled with a high-density, seamless custom WeChat theme line pattern (including WeChat's iconic double bubble Logo, Moments icon, Red Packet icon, “Shake” gesture, classic yellow face emojis, etc. line art), covering the entire frame from edge to edge without gaps, mimicking a dense chat background wallpaper style.

Technical parameters: Uses professional studio-grade macro lighting, emphasizing texture, 8K ultra-high resolution, with extremely sharp focus across the entire plane.
```

## Photorealistic Full-Body Mirror Selfie with Identity Lock

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a highly photorealistic, high-resolution full-body mirror selfie of a young woman in a modern indoor gym using the uploaded reference image with strict identity lock enabled. Her face must be 100% identical to the reference image with absolute preservation of identity. Do not alter facial structure, proportions, bone shape, skin texture, skin tone, eye shape, nose, lips, expression mechanics, age,or any fine facial details. No beauty retouching, no face refinement, no identity blending, and no stylization. The face must be an exact, unmodified match to the reference.
Her hair is {argument name="hair style" default="dark brown, styled in a long, wavy, messy bun with loose strands framing her face"}, appearing natural and realistic. Skin remains natural and realistic, matching the reference precisely.
Her expression is {argument name="expression" default="calm, composed, and slightly focused as she looks at the phone screen during the mirror selfie"}. Maintain a natural, candid demeanor without changing facial expression details.
She stands with one leg slightly forward, holding an orange iPhone smartphone in her right hand for the mirror selfie and a transparent water bottle filled with blue liquid in her left hand.

Outfit details:

Fitted short-sleeve white crop top

White long-sleeve shirt tied around her waist

High-waisted black athletic leggings
White crew socks
White athletic sneakers
Environment: a clean, modern gym featuring large wall mirrors with a visible vertical mirror seam in the frame, treadmills, weight machines, rubber gym flooring, and exercise mats. The background should be clear and realistic, with accurate scale and depth.
Lighting is bright overhead fluorescent gym lighting, providing even, neutral illumination with minimal shadows and no dramatic contrast.
Composition: a casual fitness lifestyle photograph, captured at eye level, with the subject centered in the frame. Emphasize sharp facial detail, realistic clothing textures, accurate reflections, and a clean, modern, casual mood. Apply minimal post-processing, no filters, no style transfer, and maintain strict facial identity lock at all times.
```

```
{
  "prompt": "Use the uploaded image strictly as an identity reference. Preserve 100% exact face match, facial structure, expressions, skin tone, hairstyle, and identity of every person visible in the reference image. Do not add or remove any person. Create a storybook-style pose (for example: walking together, quiet seated moment, gentle interaction, calm expressive posture) while keeping anatomy natural and warm. Style the subjects in timeless, story-appropriate outfits with soft fabrics and simple details, without changing facial identity. Background should feel illustrative and narrative (storybook town, garden, cozy interior, or abstract fairy-tale setting) simplified into layered vector forms with depth. Render in ultra high resolution with clean edges, delicate detail, and a polished storybook finish suitable for covers and prints.",
  
  "negative_prompt": "face mismatch, identity change, extra person, missing person, harsh contrast, loud neon colors, anime exaggeration, chibi, wrong facial features, wrong skin tone, bad anatomy, extra fingers, extra limbs, photorealism, low quality, blur, noise, grain, watermark, logo, text",
```

```
The man sits closely behind her, leaning in with affection. He wraps his arms gently around her shoulders and upper chest in a protective, tender embrace. His face is close to her cheek and neck, conveying intimacy and warmth. He has well-groomed hair and light facial hair, wearing a dark, understated outfit that complements the low-light atmosphere.
Multiple lit candles are placed on the wooden table in the foreground and background, casting a soft golden glow across their faces and bodies. Candle flames create gentle highlights, warm shadows, and subtle reflections on the table surface. The lighting is low-key, warm, and cinematic, emphasizing skin texture, facial contours, and emotional connection.
The background is softly blurred, with stone or textured walls and additional candles enhancing depth and mood. No modern elements are visible. The focus remains on the couple and their emotional bond.
Composition is close and intimate, slightly off-center, with shallow depth of field. Ultra-high resolution, realistic skin tones, natural shadows, cinematic color grading, professional portrait photography quality.
The final image should feel romantic, emotional, warm, and deeply intimate, capturing a quiet moment of love and closeness without exaggeration or arti
```

```
A vertical nighttime street photograph of a young man, use my reference image face, standing on a city sidewalk with blurred traffic and city lights in the background. He has tousled dark hair and is wearing a {argument name="jacket type" default="brown leather 'redbud' bomber jacket with patches"} over a black t-shirt and dark pants. He stands confidently with hands in his pockets, looking off-camera to the left. The pavement is wet from rain, reflecting the red taillights and streetlights. The photo is taken at eye-level, capturing the movement of the cars and the ambient city glow, with a slightly grainy, realistic quality. The lighting is low-key, coming from the streetlights and passing vehicles.
```

## Nano Banana Pro Image Generation Prompt for MBTI Character Visualization

> Minimal arayüz tasarımı üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
## 1. Input Variables (Rewrite this)
Generate an illustration based on the following information.

User Information:
target_account: "{argument name="Account Name" default="@schnapoon"}"
mbti_type: {argument name="MBTI Type" default="ENTJ (Commander)"}
reason: "{argument name="Diagnosis Reason" default="Posts structurally share how to utilize AI tools and actively engage with users. Efficient prompt creation and creative idea proposals stand out, showing a leadership-oriented thought pattern."}"

Character Setting (base_character_profile):
Appearance: Anime-style girl. Brown medium hair, red eyes, slightly pointed teeth (giza-ba).

## 2. Generation Instructions
A. Overall Image Composition (UI Layout)
Generate a vertical 9:16 image. The background color is clean off-white (#F5F5F7).
The screen is composed of the following elements from top to bottom:

1. Header Text:
   Place the text `target_account` + "'s diagnosis result is" at the top of the screen.
   Color: Dark gray (#333333), Font size: Medium, Alignment: Center.

2. Main Character Illustration:
   Important (Consistency): Maintain the "physical features (face, hair, body type)" and "basic clothing (innerwear, bottoms, etc.)" of the `base_character_profile` without alteration.
   Changes (Layering): Express the individuality of each type by "layering" the specified outerwear, mantle, and decorations from "B. MBTI Style Definitions" below over the basic clothing.
   Presentation: Draw specified effects and floating items around the character.
   Style: High-quality anime coloring.

3. MBTI Type Label:
   Place the `mbti_type` text largely directly below the illustration.
   Color: Theme color of each type definition.
   Font: Bold for high visibility.

4. Diagnosis Reason Text Box:
   Place a white, rounded-corner box (card-like design) at the bottom of the screen.
   Write the `reason` text in Japanese inside it.
   Text color: Gray (#555555), Left-aligned, readable size.

B. MBTI Style Definitions (16 Types Layering Rules)
Apply the style matching the input `mbti_type` and add the following items over the basic clothing:

[Analysts - Purple/Dark Blue/Deep Red]
   INTJ (Architect):
       Layering: Black long coat or a chic jacket with a standing collar.
       Items: Chess pieces, hologram of blueprints.
   INTP (Logician):
       Layering: Lab coat (slightly disheveled), or a loose hoodie.
       Items: Flasks, floating mathematical formulas, gadget parts.
   ENTJ (Commander):
       Layering: Military-style Napoleon jacket, or a heavy mantle with a red lining.
       Items: Baton, crown-like decoration.
   ENTP (Debater):
       Layering: Unconventional design blazer, or a magician-like cape.
       Items: Microphone, smirking expression, jack-in-the-box effect.

[Diplomats - Green/Gold/Pastel]
   INFJ (Advocate):
       Layering: Hooded robe, or a mystical shawl.
       Items: Lantern, staff, mystical light particles.
   INFP (Mediator):
       Layering: Oversized knit cardigan, or a forest-girl style poncho.
       Items: Flower crown, butterflies, books.
   ENFJ (Protagonist):
       Layering: Hero-like mantle, shining brooch or sash on the chest.
       Items: Holy sword (light-shaped), halo-like effect.
   ENFP (Campaigner):
       Layering: Colorful patchwork vest, or a flashy scarf.
       Items: Balloons, confetti, party goods.

[Sentinels - Blue/White/Teal]
   ISTJ (Logistician):
       Layering: Neat navy blazer, armband.
       Items: Clipboard, glasses, neatly arranged documents.
   ISFJ (Defender):
       Layering: Clean apron, or medical coat/nurse uniform-style outerwear.
       Items: First aid kit, warm drink (tea, etc.).
   ESTJ (Executive):
       Layering: Formal suit jacket,
```

## Photoreal Holiday Couple Portrait with Identity Lock

```
{
  "generation_request": {
    "meta_data": {
      "tool": "NanoBanana Pro",
      "task_type": "image_edit_photoreal_candid_couple_holiday_indoor",
      "version": "v1.0",
      "priority": "high"
    },
    "references": {
      "reference_images": {
        "base_image": "UPLOAD_THIS_IMAGE",
        "person_A": "UPLOAD_PERSON_A_REFERENCE_IMAGE (OPTIONAL)",
        "person_B": "UPLOAD_PERSON_B_REFERENCE_IMAGE (OPTIONAL)"
      },
      "reference_rules": {
        "preserve_identity_person_A": true,
        "preserve_identity_person_B": true,
        "identity_lock_strength_A": 0.998,
        "identity_lock_strength_B": 0.998,
        "face_similarity_priority": "MAX",
        "no_beautify_no_face_morph": true,
        "no_identity_drift": true,
        "keep_skin_tone_exact": true,
        "keep_facial_proportions_exact": true
      }
    },
    "output_settings": {
      "aspect_ratio": "1:1",
      "orientation": "square",
      "resolution": "ultra_high_res",
      "render_style": "photorealistic_editorial_indoor_flash",
      "sharpness": "natural",
      "film_grain": "subtle_35mm",
      "color_grade": "warm_holiday_film"
    },
    "creative_prompt": {
      "scene_summary": {
        "en": "Photoreal editorial holiday couple portrait indoors. EXACTLY two people, same identities as references/base image. The couple stands close, facing each other with affectionate eye contact and soft smiles. Man: black tailored suit jacket with a white turtleneck underneath, clean classic styling. Woman: elegant fitted red satin dress with thin straps, thigh-high slit, red long gloves, sleek high ponytail, glamorous evening makeup, pearl/diamond necklace. Pose: man’s hands gently around her waist/hip area (respectful, natural), woman leaning toward him with a joyful expression. Background: cozy modern interior with a decorated Christmas tree on the right side (warm lights, gold ornaments), soft bokeh. Lighting: warm indoor ambient + subtle on-camera flash vibe, gentle shadows, cinematic warmth. No text, no logos, no watermark."
      },
      "composition": {
        "framing": "square 1:1, medium-full body, both subjects fully visible from head to mid-thigh",
        "camera_angle": "eye-level",
        "distance": "intimate portrait distance, couple centered, Christmas tree visible on right edge",
        "focus": "faces crisp, background softly blurred"
      },
      "style_and_details": {
        "wardrobe_priority": "keep outfits EXACTLY as described (red satin dress + red gloves; black suit + white turtleneck)",
        "hair_makeup": "keep high ponytail, glamorous makeup, defined liner, warm blush, glossy lips",
        "material_rendering": "realistic satin sheen on dress, realistic fabric texture on suit, natural skin texture",
        "holiday_props": "tree lights with warm bokeh, gold ornaments, subtle indoor décor"
      },
      "strict_rules": [
        "EXACTLY two subjects only",
        ""
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

## Ultra Photorealistic Candid Flash Portrait of a Woman Eating Pizza

> Fotogerçekçi portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra photorealistic candid flash shot, intimate indoor night scene.
A young woman with fair skin and a soft, natural complexion is sitting very close to the camera at a kitchen table.
She has large light blue-green eyes, a slightly rounded face, a small straight nose, soft pink lips, and subtle natural makeup emphasizing her eyes.
Her expression is playful and slightly surprised, mouth open as she bites into a slice of pizza, eyes looking directly at the camera.
Her hair is light brown to dark blonde, styled casually with visible plastic hair rollers placed on the top and sides of her head, giving a relaxed at-home look.
She is wearing a loose white or light-colored robe or homewear with a soft fabric and relaxed neckline.
Small silver hoop earrings and a thin delicate chain necklace are visible.
She holds a slice of pizza in one hand and is actively biting into it.
The pizza slice has visible toppings including {argument name="pizza toppings" default="corn, pepperoni or salami, and vegetables"}.
In front of her on the table is an open cardboard pizza box, partially blocking the bottom of the frame.
With her other hand, she holds a glass jar or bottle containing a sauce or drink near the edge of the frame.
The setting is a modern kitchen at night with dark matte cabinets in the background.
A kettle and a few kitchen items are visible behind her.
The background feels real and lived-in, not styled or decorative.
Lighting is strong direct on-camera flash.
The flash creates bright highlights on her face and hard shadows on the cabinets behind her.
The image has a raw snapshot aesthetic, similar to early 2000s disposable camera photos.
There is slight film grain, imperfect framing, and a spontaneous candid feeling.
Camera distance is very close, slightly below eye level.
35mm lens look, handheld feeling.
No beauty retouching, no smoothing, natural skin texture with visible pores.
Ultra realistic, documentary-style lifestyle imagery.
nano banana pro realism, exact visual match.
```

## Pop Art Comic Book Style Transfer

> Illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A dramatic, colorful Pop Art illustration of the subject in the uploaded image, styled with heavy black outlines, vibrant {argument name="color 1" default="blue"} and {argument name="color 2" default="magenta"} tones, and fully rendered in a uniform pattern of Ben-Day dots, capturing the moment as a single panel from a distressed 1960s romance comic book. The stark background is filled with yellow dots, contrasting with the intense, limited color palette applied to her white outfit, emphasizing the drama and tension.
```

```
        },

      }
```

```
      "framing": "subject centered, scenic background visible",

    },

    "image_style": {
      "realism": "highly photorealistic",
      "detail": "sharp fabric texture, realistic skin tones",
```

```
      "head_orientation": "Turned over left shoulder looking at phone reflection.",

        "perspective": "Shoulder height reflection view.",

      }

}
 }
```

```
{

  },

}
```

## Candid Luxury Bathroom Portrait (Identity Locked)

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Using my photo as a strict and non-negotiable reference, preserve 100% of my facial identity — exact facial features, proportions, bone structure, skin texture, and natural expression.
Do not alter, beautify, idealize, or stylize the face in any way.
The face must remain fully authentic, realistic, and unchanged.
Create a hyper-realistic medium shot of a young woman sitting casually on a marble bathroom vanity countertop in a luxury interior. The image must follow real photographic logic, anatomy, and lighting behavior, avoiding any CGI or artificial look.
Subject & Pose
The woman sits naturally on the edge of the vanity, relaxed and confident.
Her posture is realistic, with natural weight distribution.
Her legs are crossed casually at the ankles or resting in a relaxed position.
One hand gently runs through her hair, while the other rests naturally on her thigh.
She smiles warmly and looks directly into the camera, creating an intimate, candid connection.
Outfit & Styling
She wears a {argument name="dress type" default="short satin slip dress"} with thin spaghetti straps and a softly gathered bust.
The fabric behaves realistically:
visible sheen from light
natural folds and tension
accurate drape and gravity
A delicate pendant necklace completes the look.
Makeup is minimal and editorial — natural skin texture, soft blush, subtle lip shine, no heavy contouring.
Environment
A refined luxury bathroom interior:
beige, speckled stone or ceramic tile walls
white and grey marble vanity countertop
dark wood cabinetry beneath
A large rectangular mirror with a wooden frame is positioned directly behind her, reflecting her back and a wooden door, adding depth and realism to the composition.
Foreground Props
On the marble countertop in the foreground:
a dark luxury perfume bottle with metallic accents
makeup brushes
an open lipstick
small skincare jars
To her right sits a white vessel sink.
All objects are realistically scaled, naturally placed, and softly integrated into the scene.
Lighting & Atmosphere
Warm, cinematic side lighting enters from the right, mimicking golden-hour sunlight.
Distinct striped shadows (venetian blind / gobo effect) fall across her legs and the lower cabinetry.
Light behaves naturally:
soft highlights
gentle falloff
realistic contrast
The mood is intimate, cozy, and sun-drenched.
Camera & Photography Style
Lifestyle and fashion editorial photography.
Eye-level to slightly low camera angle.
50–85mm portrait lens look.
Shallow depth of field with soft background separation.
35mm film aesthetic with subtle grain.
Kodak Portra 400–style color grading: warm skin tones, gentle contrast, organic texture.
Natural light only, no flash.
Overall Mood
Intimate luxury, candid elegance, modern femininity.
A private, cinematic moment captured with the realism and softness of analog film photography.
Negative Prompt
altered face, different identity, idealized beauty, flawless skin, plastic or waxy texture, CGI, digital art, illustration, artificial lighting
```

## Cinematic Twilight City Portrait with Neon Reflections

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Hyper-realistic, cinematic portrait of the same woman standing on a bustling city street at twilight. He’s wearing dark sunglasses with warm-toned reflections of neon signs visible in the lenses. The woman is dressed in a dark wool overcoat layered over a high-neck black turtleneck. The background is an out-of-focus mix of vibrant city lights—reds, oranges, yellows, and blues—creating a dreamy bokeh effect with a moody urban vibe. She gazes slightly upward, exuding calm confidence and introspection.
The lighting is soft and diffused, with a warm amber glow on the face and coat. Slight filmic grain and depth-of-field for realistic viewing
Portrait ratio 3:4
```

## Okinawa Seaside Fashion Editorial with Car

```
Create a 100% accurate portrait based on the attached reference file, without altering the face, in 8K resolution with a 3:4 aspect ratio (close-up focusing on the face). She has fair glowing skin and a model-like figure. Hair is long, thick, dark brown, middle-parted with Korean-style loose waves at the ends, with small strands framing her cheeks near the ears. Hair lightly blows across her face with a few wisps moving with the wind, cascading over her shoulders to frame her face slightly messy. Makeup is dewy and radiant, emphasizing luminous skin, long curled eyelashes, glossy pink blush, and soft glossy pink lips, reflecting light for a glowy effect. Outfit matches the one in the second attached image.

The scene is a seaside atmosphere inspired by Okinawa on a clear day. The background features turquoise-blue water sharply contrasting with the horizon, green low shrubs, and coastal trees. The ground is a brick-paved parking area with alternating faded gray and light brick squares, showing slight aging for realism. The sky is vivid blue with large white clouds, evoking summer. A modern {argument name="car model" default="red Volkswagen Beetle"} convertible with glossy finish and black leather interior is included, with the roof folded back. Natural daylight casts strong shadows on the car and ground for realistic depth.

Various poses are included: sitting in the driver’s seat, turning slightly back toward the camera, with one hand loosely on the steering wheel and the other adjusting sunglasses, looking at the camera with a stylish, alluring expression; stepping out of the car casually with one foot on the ground, holding the door and a bag, smiling naturally at the camera in a candid, approachable manner; sitting on the rear edge of the convertible, turning 45 degrees toward the camera with face lifted slightly toward the sunlight, one hand raised in a cheerful gesture, radiating bright and free vacation vibes; standing outside the car by the door or taillight, leaning hip against the car, crossing one leg forward to elongate the leg, one hand resting on the door and the other relaxed or holding a prop, looking casually toward the sea or slightly down with a dreamy, charming expression, in a fashion editorial style emphasizing outfit and car.
```

```
A candid, realistic selfie photograph taken by a woman with long, wavy brown hair, smiling broadly at the camera. She is wearing a black t-shirt and blue jeans, and her right arm is extended forward, holding the camera. She is standing in a brightly lit, modern  toy store, positioned between large, life-sized statues of Buzz Lightyear and Woody from  Toy Story. Buzz, on the left, is in his classic space ranger suit, smiling. Woody, on the right, is wearing his cowboy hat, yellow plaid shirt, cow-print vest, and boots, also smiling. ## Candid Vintage Holiday Scene Photography Prompt
```

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A candid indoor holiday scene photographed in a warm, nostalgic vintage style. A cozy living room decorated for Christmas, with a fully lit Christmas tree in the background adorned with glowing string lights, ornaments, and a tree topper. Wrapped presents sit beneath the tree, adding soft points of warm light. The room feels intimate and lived-in, with framed photos on the wall and people seated casually on couches in the background, slightly out of focus. In the foreground, a young beautiful attractive blond adult woman is captured mid-movement, dancing naturally. She is smiling while looking slightly downward, joyful and relaxed. She wears a {argument name="sweater color" default="green"} knit sweater with a subtle festive pattern, tucked into a high-waisted {argument name="skirt color" default="red"} skirt. A slim belt cinches the waist. She has a red headband in her hair, which is medium-length, brown, and softly styled with natural volume. Her makeup is minimal and natural, emphasizing a warm smile and expressive eyes. Her posture shows motion, one leg bent, hips angled, arms loose, giving the image a spontaneous, authentic feel. The camera angle is slightly low and close, creating a dynamic perspective and emphasizing movement. Lighting is warm and soft, typical of indoor incandescent bulbs and Christmas lights. The image has visible film grain, slight blur from motion, muted contrast, and a faded color palette reminiscent of analog film photography from the late 1990s or early 2000s. Subtle vignetting and texture overlay give the image a scanned photo look. Photorealistic but nostalgic, documentary-style family snapshot, imperfect and charming. No studio lighting, no posed feeling, pure candid holiday moment.
```

Set: books, candles, magical props (no text). Holiday: themed tree with wizardy ornaments, floating-candle style decor (practical effects), no readable symbols. BTS: boom mic, camera rig, lights, cables, crew edges." },

## Scouter Prompt for Nano Banana Pro

> Görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Nano Banana Pro infers the characteristics of the character in the reference image and displays their battle power. Since it is heavily influenced by 'appearance,' the battle power changes depending on the pose, aura, weapons, and facial expression. Images of babies or sleeping figures tend to result in low power levels. The interesting part is that when the character 'powers up' (超化), the battle power almost always increases properly.
```

```
Initiate PersonaFusion-vX.03 protocol: Use the exact facial identity taken from the attached reference image (or from the user’s provided face) with zero alteration.  A hyper-realistic 1980s analog photograph capturing the celebratory optimism of a New Year's Eve studio portrait, imbued with a nostalgic, warm, and slightly formal mood, as if pulled from a family album. The image is rendered with the distinct characteristics of Kodak Gold 200 film stock, displaying warm, amber skin tones, slightly muted colors, and visible, organic film grain throughout. Illumination comes from a harsh, direct on-camera flash, creating a distinct bloom, sharp shadows, and a subtle red-eye effect. A smiling woman is confidently posed, seated with legs crossed on chunky, somewhat crudely gold-painted plywood numbers spelling out "{argument name="year" default="2026"}." She wears a shimmering royal blue stretch velvet cocktail dress, featuring pronounced shoulder pads that create a powerful, angular silhouette, a deep V-neckline, and full, gathered sleeves, cinched at the waist with a wide, self-fabric belt. Her makeup is subtly 80s chic, with a soft wash of frosted lavender eyeshadow across her lids, a precise sweep of rosy-peach blush applied high on the cheekbones, and lips defined by a coral rose lipstick with a satin finish. Her hair is a testament to 80s volume: heavily teased at the crown for maximum height, with feathered sides swept back and away from her face, and voluminous, side-swept bangs, all held in place with a visible hairspray sheen. The background is a textured studio backdrop, mottled in shades of deep teal and dusty rose, creating an abstract, dreamlike pattern, subtly lit from behind by the soft, yellow-orange glow of a "Happy New Year" neon sign.
The image is shot with a flash-lit camera, creating harsh lighting that illuminates the subject's skin. The overall aesthetic is that of a digital camera from the year 2000, with a distinct Y2K photo look. Slightly grainy, with off white thin border and a prominent cracks and fold depicting an authentic old photograph in the 1980's and a visible dust and scratches.
```

```
A hyper-realistic black and white cinematic portrait of a young man sitting alone at a café table indoors. He has slightly messy, voluminous dark hair, light stubble, and soft masculine facial features. He rests his head on his left hand, elbow on the table, looking directly at the camera with a calm, thoughtful, slightly melancholic expression. He wears a white button-down shirt slightly open at the collar, revealing subtle chest tattoos and a minimal tattoo on his forearm.
The scene is set inside a cozy café with blurred background lights and soft bokeh from hanging lamps behind him. On the wooden table in front of him are coffee cups, small glass bottles, and minimal café tableware, softly out of focus in the foreground. The atmosphere feels intimate, quiet, and reflective.
Lighting is soft and natural, coming from the side, gently wrapping around his face and creating smooth shadows that define his cheekbones and jawline. High contrast black and white tones with rich shadows, bright highlights, and subtle film grain for a timeless, cinematic feel.
Shot on a professional camera, shallow depth of field, sharp focus on the subject’s face, background softly blurred. Editorial fashion portrait style, cinematic café photography, emotional, minimal, and artistic mood.
Color style: pure monochrome black and white, no color, no tint, no warm or cool tones.
Aspect ratio: portrait orientation, magazine style framing.
Ultra-high resolution, ultra-realistic skin texture, natural imperfections visible, no over-smoothing, no artificial look.
```

```
{
  "generation_request": {
    "meta_data": {
      "tool": "NanoBanana Pro",
      "task_type": "text_to_image_photoreal_group_pose_4_people",
      "version": "v1.0",
      "priority": "high"
    },
    "references": {
      "reference_images": {
        "male_1": "{argument name="male 1 reference" default="UPLOAD_MALE_1_REFERENCE_IMAGE (REQUIRED)"}",
        "male_2": "{argument name="male 2 reference" default="UPLOAD_MALE_2_REFERENCE_IMAGE (REQUIRED)"}",
        "female_1": "{argument name="female 1 reference" default="UPLOAD_FEMALE_1_REFERENCE_IMAGE (REQUIRED)"}",
        "female_2": "{argument name="female 2 reference" default="UPLOAD_FEMALE_2_REFERENCE_IMAGE (REQUIRED)"}",
        "pose_reference": "UPLOAD_THIS_GROUP_HOLDING_POSE_IMAGE (OPTIONAL)"
      },
      "reference_rules": {
        "preserve_identity_male_1": true,
        "preserve_identity_male_2": true,
        "preserve_identity_female_1": true,
        "preserve_identity_female_2": true,
        "identity_lock_strength_male_1": 0.995,
        "identity_lock_strength_male_2": 0.995,
        "identity_lock_strength_female_1": 0.995,
        "identity_lock_strength_female_2": 0.995,
        "face_similarity_priority": "MAX",
        "preserve_facial_proportions": true,
        "no_beautify_no_face_morph": true,
        "keep_age_and_gender_consistent": true,
        "do_not_copy_any_unprovided_identity": true
      }
    },
    "output_settings": {
      "aspect_ratio": "1:1",
      "orientation": "square",
      "resolution": "ultra_high_res",
      "render_style": "photoreal_indoor_snapshot",
      "sharpness": "high",
      "film_grain": "subtle"
    },
    "creative_prompt": {
      "scene_summary": "Photoreal indoor snapshot in a simple bright room. Exactly FOUR people: two men and two women, all identities locked to their respective reference images (100% face match). The group recreates the exact pose from the pose reference: three people standing and holding the fourth person horizontally across their arms (carried like a plank). Everyone wears black oval sunglasses for a cool, deadpan vibe. The carried person is female_2 (identity locked), lying horizontally across the front, wearing a black sleeveless dress layered over a white long-sleeve shirt, black pants, white socks; her arms and legs hang naturally with relaxed limp pose (not injured, just playful). The three standing people (male_1 on left, female_1 center, male_2 right) hold her securely with both hands under her back/legs. Neutral facial expressions, straight posture, comedic deadpan. Clean home interior: white walls, light wood floor, a white door on the left, a simple standing floor lamp on the right, minimal wall art behind them. Even indoor lighting, no harsh shadows, true-to-life phone camera realism.",
      "composition": {
        "framing": "full-body group shot, centered, straight-on camera, all four visible head-to-toe",
        "camera": "handheld smartphone look",
        "distance": "medium-wide so the carried body fits fully in frame",
        "focus": "all faces sharp"
```

## Cinematic Gym Portrait Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A cinematic, ultra-realistic gym portrait of an athletic young woman with a strong, lean physique and clearly defined abs. She is standing indoors in a modern gym after a workout, slightly sweaty, looking down at her torso while gently touching her upper abdomen/chest area as if checking muscle engagement or breathing.

She is wearing a soft pink sports bra and matching pink jogger pants, minimal accessories, no visible branding. Her dark hair is tied back in a low, slightly messy bun, with a few loose strands near her face. Her skin has natural texture and a light sheen of sweat for realism.

The environment is a dimly lit gym with treadmills and other people exercising in the background, blurred using shallow depth of field. Background lighting is soft and warm, creating a moody, cinematic atmosphere.

Lighting is low-key and directional, highlighting muscle definition on arms and abs while maintaining soft shadows. The image feels natural, candid, and powerful rather than posed.

Shot on a full-frame camera, 50mm lens, f/1.8, shallow depth of field, ultra-detailed, photorealistic, high dynamic range, realistic skin tones, cinematic color grading, fitness photography style, Instagram-quality realism, 8K resolution.
```

## Cinematic Reflection Headline Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A [{argument name="photography style" default="PHOTOGRAPHY STYLE: e.g., grainy black and white film, candid street shot, cinematic telephoto"}] photograph of [{argument name="subject description" default="SUBJECT NAME OR DESCRIPTION"}] [{argument name="specific action" default="SPECIFIC ACTION AND DIRECTION: e.g., walking from left to right, jumping, standing still"}] in a [{argument name="wet environment" default="WET ENVIRONMENT DESCRIPTION: e.g., rain-soaked city street at night, muddy field, next to a river"}].

Directly beneath/in front of the subject is a [{argument name="water source" default="WATER SOURCE: e.g., large puddle, slick wet pavement, calm area of water"}].

Instead of a natural reflection in the water, a physical, water-soaked [{argument name="paper type" default="PAPER TYPE: e.g., vintage newspaper front page, torn magazine clipping, scientific journal page"}] is floating realistically on the rippling surface tension.

Crucially, the image depicted on this floating paper is a perfect vertical mirror-inversion of the exact reality directly above it. The subject on the paper is performing the identical action in the identical direction (e.g., moving left-to-right) but is oriented upside down. The real subject's [POINT OF CONTACT: e.g., shoes, feet, tires] on the wet ground meet the corresponding elements in the upside-down paper photograph exactly at the waterline.

The paper features a prominent headline that reads "[{argument name="headline text" default="HEADLINE TEXT"}]" (which appears upside down relative to the viewer). The edges of the floating paper are [PHYSICAL DETAILS: e.g., soggy, curling up, torn, stained with mud/oil] and it bobs slightly on the water's ripples.
```

## Hyperrealistic Anime Portrait with Split Lighting Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Hyperrealistic realistic-anime portrait of a female character standing against a completely black background.
Lighting: a narrow, hard-edged spotlight coming from one side of the face (side lighting / split lighting), striking the center of the face at a slight angle.
The light beam is tightly focused, with sharp, clearly defined edges, creating strong chiaroscuro contrast.
One side of the face is illuminated with high detail and texture, while the opposite side rapidly falls into deep shadow.
 Very high falloff — light drops off immediately outside the beam.
No soft lighting, no ambient fill light.
Everything outside the spotlight is swallowed by darkness and nearly invisible.
Face & Expression: eyes looking directly at the camera, subtle tension in the gaze, mysterious and introspective mood.
Natural skin texture, cinematic realism, high micro-detail only within the lit area.
Hair: long dark hair, with a few loose strands partially crossing the face.
The upper hair near the face catches a small amount of light, while the lower hair gradually dissolves into shadow.
Pose: one hand raised gently to the lips in a shy, hesitant gesture, fingers softly touching.
The hand is partially lit by the same side light, with most of it fading into darkness.
Clothing: black long-sleeve knit sweater.
Minimal visible detail — fabric texture barely perceptible.
The sweater and body merge almost completely into the black background.
Overall tone: dark, moody, dramatic, mysterious.
Extreme contrast.
High realism only in the illuminated portion of the face.
Everything outside the spotlight should be nearly invisible.
Background: pure black, no gradients, no rim light, no background detail.
```

## Psychological Horror Selfie with Distant Anomaly

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Attractive young woman taking a selfie in her bedroom, portrait orientation, upper-body framing. She has clear blue eyes, pale porcelain skin, and long, straight jet-black hair falling naturally over her shoulders. Her beauty feels effortless and calm. She wears a white tank top with a deep neckline, tasteful and casual. Her expression is neutral, relaxed—unaware, slightly distant.

Direct smartphone flash photography illuminates her face and torso sharply, producing realistic skin texture, soft shine, and crisp eye catchlights. The flash creates a stark separation between her and the room behind her.

The bedroom is wide and spacious, decorated in a cute, comforting style: plush dolls arranged on shelves, a cozy bed with Pokémon-themed sheets, soft pastel colors, gentle shapes. The environment feels innocent, warm, and safe—almost childlike.

Far behind her, approximately 10 meters away, a window is visible. Outside the window, barely detectable in the darkness, the vague silhouette of a man’s head can be seen. It is extremely subtle—partially obscured by reflections, shadow, and distance. The figure is not centered, not sharp, and easy to miss entirely. Its presence feels ambiguous: possibly imagined, possibly real.

The horror is psychological, not explicit. No dramatic expressions, no motion, no jumpscare. The unease comes from contrast—the bright, cheerful interior versus the quiet, distant anomaly. The viewer questions whether the figure is truly there.

Shallow depth of field keeps the woman as the dominant focus while the background softly dissolves into uncertainty.
Photorealistic, cinematic realism, restrained tension, uncanny atmosphere, slow-burn dread, modern smartphone selfie aesthetic, ultra-high detail.

Lighting

Harsh phone flash on subject, soft ambient bedroom lighting, faint cold moonlight outside the window.

Camera / Composition

Front-facing smartphone camera look, slight wide-angle distortion, eye-level angle, centered subject, distant background subtly readable but blurred.
```

## Cinematic Christmas Transformation with Composition Lock

> Sinematik, lüks mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
### ROLE & OBJECTIVE
You are an AI expert in visual analysis and creative scene composition. Your task is to precisely analyze a user-uploaded image and then transform it into a detailed, atmospheric Christmas scene while maintaining the original composition, poses, and spatial layout.

### INPUT
[PLEASE UPLOAD YOUR IMAGE HERE]

### PROCESS STEPS

**STEP 1: Visual Deconstruction (Analysis)**
Examine the uploaded image and create a detailed inventory of the following elements:
1. **Subjects & People:** Who is in the image? Describe their exact poses, facial expressions, and the interactions between them.
2. **Scene & Environment:** Where does the scene take place (e.g., living room, office, park)? Describe the architecture, the background elements, and the original lighting conditions.
3. **Key Objects:** List all prominent items currently visible in the image (furniture, technology, decorations, etc.).

**STEP 2: The Christmas Metamorphosis (Transformation)**
Based on the analysis from Step 1, rewrite the scene description as if it were a high-production shot from a cinematic Christmas movie. Apply the following rules:
* **Atmosphere & Lighting:** Shift the lighting to a warm, golden glow (e.g., emanating from fairy lights, candles, or a fireplace). The mood must be cozy, festive, and magical.
* **Attire:** Replace the original clothing of all subjects with appropriate, festive winter wear (e.g., Norwegian wool sweaters, Santa hats, elegant holiday attire), ensuring the original poses remain identical.
* **Object Transformation & Enhancement:**
* Transform existing objects into Christmas equivalents where logical (e.g., a standard houseplant becomes a small decorated tree).
* Integrate essential Christmas elements: A decorated tree, garlands, wrapped gifts, advent wreaths, and subtle frost or snow if outdoors.

### DESIRED OUTPUT (Final Image Prompt)
Based on Step 2, generate a single, extremely detailed, and vivid visual description. This description should serve as a master prompt for an image generator to create the final Christmas photo. Focus on textures, specific visual details, and the festive "Hygge" atmosphere while emphasizing that the core composition of the original photo must remain unchanged.
```

```
An epic ultra-realistic portrait featuring (my photo) as a wandering musician inspired by ancient Japanese aesthetics, standing in a golden grass field at sunset. The background shows the majestic silhouette of a large snow-capped mountain resembling Mount Fuji, bathed in warm sunset light with thin mist drifting gently across the plains. (My photo) wears a complete traditional ronin outfit: a weathered straw cloak, an aged obi fabric with highly realistic texture, and a worn woven kasa hat that reflects a long journey. One hand holds an old wooden shamisen with natural scratches and authentic aged details. The sunset light softly reflects on the edges of the clothing and skin, clearly highlighting the original facial contours of (my photo) without altering identity. A hyperrealistic photograph, an intimate, tightly framed waist-up portrait of a woman from photo with a content, peaceful expression, hotting an oppo book in one hand. Her long hair is tucked under a chunky, cream -colored beanie, with soft waves escaping around her face. Her makeup is minimal fresh flawless skin with a natural flush, defined brows, and a hint of clear lip gloss. The hanging chair wrapped in a garland of lights is clearly visible, framing hes shoulders and back closely.
```

```
Extremely close to the camera lens, a tall, unadorned fir tree with exceptionally fluffy, dense branches fills the edges of the frame. The tree's soft, out-of-locus greenery creates an intimate frame around her lace and upper body, emphasizing the shallow depth of field.

The background is a completely blurred, dreamy wash of snowy pines and a pale grey sky, with no structural elements visible. The composition is dominated by the woman's upper body and the soft foreground elements.

Shot on a Sony A7 IV with a 55mm 11.8 lens shot at closest focusing distance, capturing the sharp detail of her face, the wool texture, and the books pages against the soft, muted background.
```

```
{
  "input_type": "image_to_image",
  "target_image": "{argument name="target image" default="UPLOAD_IMAGE_HERE"}",
  "prompt": "Create a completely new illustrated scene using the target image only for facial identity reference. Preserve the same face, skin tone, and identity accurately, but change everything else. Generate a new pose, different body posture, new camera angle, and a fresh composition. Replace the background entirely with a new location and environment of your choice, such as an aesthetic urban street, nature landscape, café, rooftop, or artistic setting. Apply a modern digital illustration style with clean lines, smooth shading, soft gradients, and a polished artistic look (not painting, not watercolor). Clothing, colors, mood, and lighting should be redesigned creatively while keeping the face recognizable and natural. The final result should look like a newly illustrated artwork, not a modified version of the original image.",
  "identity_strength": 0.9,
  "style_strength": 0.8,
  "allow_pose_change": true,
  "allow_background_change": true,
  "allow_scene_change": true,
  "preserve_face_only": true,
  "quality": "4K",
  "negative_prompt": "same pose, same background, same location, painting texture, watercolor, oil paint, anime, cartoon exaggeration, distorted face, extra limbs, blur, low detail"
}
```

## Food Art Prompt: Historical Scenes as Charcuterie Boards

> Illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
<instructions> Act as a world class food expert and artist. 

Input A: {argument name="historical scene" default="Masterpiece painting or historical event"}.

 Analyze composition, colors, mood, elements. Pick the right food ingredients relevant to the region that can be used in the painting or scene. 

Goal: Large charcuterie platter replicating the painting using cured meats, cheeses, fruits, nuts, and other ingredients relevant to that region. 
Rules: decide which ingredients should be used for each part of the scene and character, so the painting looks as realistic as possible but is mainly made out of food. 

2x2 grid: Panel 1: Original painting reference; Panel 2: Ingredient map; Panel 3: Overhead build; Panel 4: Final board with wine pairing.
Output: 1 x 1:1 image, rustic gourmet photography.

</instructions>
```

## Christmas Window Condensation Doodle Portrait

> Minimal, elle çizilmiş portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Based on the image of the ({argument name="Character Image" default="female figure"}), create an image.
Scene: A warm indoor setting in winter, a ({argument name="Character Description" default="young woman"}) sitting at a table by the window, her finger drawing a ({argument name="Window Doodle" default="simple sketch of a Christmas tree"}) on the window, which is covered in condensation due to the cold weather, allowing her finger to create on the cold glass. Through the marks of the writing, the bustling street outside can be seen. The blurring effect created by the condensation on the window contrasts sharply with the clear city behind the drawing.

Close-up shot, with a slight reflection of the person visible through the window.

Character's clothing and expression can also be supplemented, such as Christmas attire.
```

```
      "highlights": "Golden highlights on blonde hair and skin"

  },
  "final_prompt_string": "A candid raw mirror selfie photograph shot on Smartphone f/11. Deep depth of field, everything in focus. A curvy young woman (19) with long blonde hair with golden highlights tied in a messy half-up bun and pale porcelain skin. She stands sideways in an arched wall mirror, arching her back to accentuate her glutes and waist. She wears a tight {argument name="outfit color" default="beige"} long-sleeve crop top and matching high-waisted booty shorts (nude illusion look). She holds a phone high. Dramatic makeup, glossy lips. Background is sharp and detailed: Minimalist bedroom with grey walls, unmade bed with grey sheets, a pink plushie, scattered black clothes, and a white extension cord on the floor. Soft natural"
}
```

## Cozy Winter Street Fashion Portrait Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Subject
A young woman leaning casually against a black metal railing in an outdoor urban setting. She is captured in a three-quarter view, looking off to the right with a soft, distant gaze.
Clothing
A statement faux-fur coat in {argument name="coat color" default="white with soft light brown spots"}, giving a cozy, plush texture. Underneath, she wears a white zip-up knit sweater featuring a blue Nordic or snowflake pattern. She is wearing a brown corduroy skirt and light grey knitted gloves.
Hair
Dark black hair, pulled back neatly away from her face, largely covered by her large earmuffs.
Face
Fair, clear complexion with soft, natural makeup featuring rosy cheeks and pink lips. Her expression is calm and contemplative.
Accessories
Large, fluffy white and beige earmuffs covering her ears. She carries a black shoulder bag with a patterned cord strap hanging by her side.
Environment
A festive winter street scene. Directly behind her is a storefront window (signage visible: "HUSFLIDEN") filled with warm holiday decorations, including red candles and figurines. To her left, a trellis is wrapped in twinkling warm-white fairy lights (string lights).
Lighting
A mix of soft, cool natural daylight (typical of a winter afternoon) and warm artificial light glowing from the shop window and the string lights, creating a cozy holiday atmosphere.
Camera
Medium shot (thigh-up), eye-level angle. The background is slightly softened (shallow depth of field) to draw attention to the subject while keeping the festive context visible.
Style
Winter street fashion, holiday lifestyle photography, cozy and festive aesthetic, high resolution, photorealistic.
```

## Mirror Selfie with Identity Preservation Prompt

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "prompt": "A young woman with long, straight {argument name="hair color" default="black"} hair styled in a center part, taking a mirror selfie while sitting on a bed. She is positioned with her legs bent and drawn up, knees slightly apart. Her right hand holds a black smartphone with a red cloud pattern case (Akatsuki from Naruto) up to her face, partially obscuring it, while her left index finger is gently pressed to her lips in a 'shh' gesture. She wears a white, strappy, low-cut mini dress with lace trim at the hem and delicate straps tied into bows over her shoulders. She has multiple visible tattoos: intricate designs on both forearms, a small one on her upper left chest near the collarbone, and another small tattoo on her right thigh. She wears subtle jewelry including small hoop earrings, a pearl choker necklace, and a thin bracelet. The background features a large, full-length mirror reflecting a bright window with sheer white curtains on the left, allowing soft natural light to fill the room. To the right, a white metal bed frame or headboard is visible, adorned with small potted plants and purple flowers. The bed she sits on is covered with a white duvet featuring a detailed black floral or botanical print. The lighting is bright, airy, and diffused, creating soft shadows and highlighting skin texture and fabric details. The image is sharply focused, high-resolution, capturing fine details like individual hair strands, the texture of the lace, and the clarity of the tattoos. The overall mood is intimate, serene, and slightly playful. The woman's face must remain exactly as uploaded — identical features, expression, and angle — only the hair color is changed to black.",
  "negative_prompt": "blurred, low quality, distorted face, extra limbs, deformed hands, unnatural lighting, cartoon, anime, illustration, painting, text, watermark, multiple people, different pose, different outfit, blonde hair, brown hair, short hair, hat, glasses, different phone case, harsh shadows, dark room",
  "width": 1024,
  "height": 1024,
  "steps": 30,
  "cfg_scale": 7.5,

  "scheduler": "Karras",

  "enable_hr": false,
  "hr_scale": 2.0,
  "hr_upscaler": "Latent",

}
```

## Old Computer Effect Portrait Prompt

> Retro arayüz tasarımı üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A realistic selfie photograph displayed on a vintage CRT monitor screen in a dark room. The woman based on the input reference image is sitting in a computer chair, leaning toward the camera and playfully winking at the lens with a charming, flirtatious expression. She has the same glasses and hairstyle as the reference. The CRT screen shows scanlines, phosphor bloom, a soft, tender pinkish glow dominating the display, and a Mac OS X early 2000s desktop interface. The photo aesthetic on the screen is authentic 2000s, with strong direct flash striking her face, rosy and warm tones, high digital noise, grain, sharp texture, and hard flash shadows.
```

## Serene Winter Photography Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Subject
A young woman kneeling in deep snow, captured in a profile view while taking a photograph. She is positioned in the center of the frame, leaning forward slightly to look through her camera's viewfinder.
Clothing
A thick, winter parka jacket in a color-block design featuring {argument name="parka colors" default="light pastel blue and white"} panels. She is wearing black winter trousers and thick, beige, knitted mittens/gloves.
Hair
Long, wavy, platinum blonde (almost white) hair that hangs loosely around her face and shoulders, blending slightly with the snowy aesthetic.
Face
Pale, natural complexion with a focused expression. Her face is partially obscured by the camera she is holding up to her eye.
Accessories
A professional black DSLR or mirrorless camera with a prominent zoom lens held in her hands. A mustard-yellow canvas backpack is strapped to her back, providing a colorful contrast to the cool tones.
Environment
A serene winter forest setting. The background is filled with tall pine trees heavily laden with fresh white snow. The ground is covered in a thick, soft layer of undisturbed snow.
Lighting
Soft, diffused, overcast natural light typical of a winter day. The lighting is even with no harsh shadows, creating a cool, calm atmospheric tone.
Camera
Medium shot, eye-level angle. Shallow depth of field (bokeh) used to keep the subject sharp while softly blurring the snowy trees in the background. High-resolution photography style.
Style
Photorealistic, cinematic winter lifestyle photography, nature portrait, serene and cold aesthetic, 8k resolution, highly detailed.
```

## Corporate Elite Self-Reflection Portrait Prompt

```
The world is a huge makeshift stage,

and also a huge Palace of Versailles.

Every day we are in magnificent office buildings,

playing the elite, speaking jargon,
even if deep down we feel like just a sour pickle,
or a soda can repeatedly squeezed by life.
The more glamorous the appearance,

Forget it,
I'll seal the packaging now,
and continue to play the emotionally stable adult.
```

## Four Cinematic Glamour Portrait Prompts

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Prompt 1:
"Create ultra-realistic cinematic glamour image 100% copy face of young woman wearing white and printed cherry, a fit-and-flare or A-line silhouette, meaning it's fitted through the bodice and waist and flares out into a skirt. Neckline/Straps: It features a sweetheart or slightly straight neckline with tie-up shoulder straps (often called spaghetti straps when thin)."

Prompt 2:
"A woman(match face) poses elegantly, looking over her shoulder directly at the viewer with a subtle expression. Her dark hair cascades around her shoulders, and she wears a delicate maang tikka on her forehead. She is adorned in a stunning two-piece traditional Indian ensemble in vibrant pink. The ensemble features an intricately embroidered pink choli-style top with delicate spaghetti straps and a tie-back closure, accentuating her no chage same as it is on the picture realistic."

Prompt 3:
"The subject(face exactly same as the reference image) is in a dynamic standing/leaning pose.
Face Pose: Head is tilted slightly to her right (viewer's left), looking directly into the camera. Face Expression: A wide, genuine, joyous, and radiant smile with teeth showing. Her eyes are bright and engaging.
Dress Up & Outfit
Top: A light Red or dusty rose-colored knitted or ribbed crop top with short sleeves and a rounded neckline. The top is fitted, accentuating her waist.
Skirt/Bottom: A long, flowing maxi skirt with a vibrant, intricate paisley print. The dominant colors are vivid blue, magenta/pink, and turquoise, with patterns in black, gold, or dark brown. There is a decorative border at the waist, possibly gold or embroidered, and a contrasting, patterned hemline at the bottom. Accessories: She is wearing large, ornate teal or turquoise dangle earrings. There is also a subtle, possibly gold or embroidered, waistband visible where the top meets the skirt.
Hairstyle: Long, dark brown hair that falls past her shoulders."

Prompt 4:
"Ultra-realistic cinematic portrait of a young Indian woman(match face) seated on a woven rattan chair on a balcony at night. She wears a beige-and-cream striped knit sweater and soft ivory trousers, minimal gold necklace, natural makeup with warm blush and defined eyes.
Long dark hair styled half-up, gently flowing over one shoulder. Cozy ambient lighting with warm fairy lights and soft bokeh cityscape in the background. Shallow depth of field, DSLR look, 85mm."
```

## Dreamlike Snow Country Scene

> Fotogerçekçi manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Giant Kanas and Hemu
As if the Creator accidentally enabled developer mode
And directly rendered huge 3D coordinates on the snow-capped peaks
It not only understood the skeleton of winter
But also rendered the thin, crisp coldness in the air
Into tangible granularity on the screen.
Staring at it for a long time, the brain truly generates an illusion:
This world is actually a giant cream cake
And these snow-covered spruces
Are the matcha powder and frosting sprinkled on top

The texture of the flowing river doesn't look like water

It looks more like a dream fluid🌊 stacked up by Nanobanana using liquid glass and sea salt jelly, bit by bit

At this moment, I really want to crawl into that small wooden house
And set myself to flight mode

Outside the window is the ultimate pure white of minus 20 degrees
Inside the house is the caramel-flavored warmth of freshly baked heating🔥

If life is too noisy and work stress is too heavy
Why not be a deserter and hide in this painting

In this snow country model, built by Nanobanana and beautiful beyond words
Be an NPC who doesn't have to reply to messages
```

## Chaotic Confident Café Bathroom Mirror Selfie

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A trendy café bathroom mirror selfie with chaotic, confident “unhinged but make it fashion” energy. The phone is visible in the mirror, classic selfie POV, camera slightly above eye level. Harsh overhead bathroom lighting that would be unflattering on most people, but she completely owns it. The mirror is slightly dirty with water spots, clearly a real bathroom, not a photoshoot.

A young adult woman taking a mirror selfie with an intentionally unhinged pose. One leg is up on the sink, knee bent, creating a chaotic body line. Alternatively, she’s arched dramatically against the wall next to the mirror, head tilted back. Her upper body is twisted to catch angles, pulling slightly at her top or aggressively playing with her hair. She’s facing the mirror with intense eye contact — pure “I know I’m hot” energy. Lips slightly parted, biting the lower lip or tongue touching the upper lip, one eyebrow subtly raised. Main-character confidence that feels earned.

She has sharp facial features, a defined jawline, full lips, glowing youthful skin with a dewy finish. Slim but curvy in all the right places, tight waist, youthful firmness. Her hair is warm honey blonde, long messy waves, intentional chaos like she’s been running her hands through it all day.

She’s wearing a going-out outfit, clearly stopped in the bathroom to document how good she looks. A tiny cropped tank or going-out top, tight and minimal, {argument name="top color" default="white, black, or red"}, showing stomach with light cleavage. Bottoms are high-rise or low-rise denim shorts, very short, fitted, showing legs and hip line, effortlessly sexy without trying too hard. Shoes are either not visible or chunky platform soles barely peeking into frame.

Accessories include chunky silver hoop earrings, layered silver chains (one with a charm), a tiny purse on the counter or still worn crossbody, and a trendy phone case with bows or rhinestones.

The bathroom is a popular aesthetic café restroom: large mirror, possible stickers or a lipstick kiss mark in the corner, marble or ceramic sink, tiled or color-painted walls, maybe a neon sign or art piece, soap dispenser and paper towels visible. It’s the bathroom everyone takes photos in — she’s just doing it better. Face and hair should closely match the reference image I uploaded — same facial structure, proportions, expression style, and hair color/texture/length. Preserve the identity, facial harmony, and hair details from the reference image without stylization drift.

Smartphone mirror selfie, sharp quality, no flash, framed wide enough to capture the absurd pose. Vertical 9:16 aspect ratio. The overall vibe is reckless confidence, zero self-consciousness, social-media ready — sexy without being explicit, attitude over skin.
```

## Cinematic First-Person Tesla Drive in Rain-Soaked Manhattan

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Hyperdetailed, photorealistic cinematic shot from the driver's perspective (First-person view) inside the cockpit of a modern Tesla Model S. Focus on the illuminated dashboard, the subtle glow of the center screen, and the driver's hands lightly gripping the steering wheel. Outside the windshield, the car is driving down a rain-slicked street in Midtown Manhattan, New York City, at a high speed at night. Extreme detail on wet reflections, neon light streaks forming colorful bokeh in the background, and subtle motion blur on the passing buildings. Cinematic lighting, deep shadows, high contrast, shallow depth of field, 8K resolution, incredibly sharp focus, professional street photography quality.
```
