## Casual 'Lazy-Hot' Mirror Selfie in Dorm Room

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
(Vertical 9:16 smartphone aspect ratio). A highly realistic, casual full-body mirror selfie taken in a cozy, slightly messy bedroom or college dorm.

Subject: A cute 19-year-old Caucasian girl with a youthful, fresh face and long, messy-wavy blonde/light-brown hair.
*   Physique: She has a fit, curvy body with a slim waist and wider hips.
*   Pose: She stands in front of a tall floor mirror. She adopts a flattering pose: one leg is stepped slightly forward, and she pops her hip to the side, accentuating her curves. She holds an iPhone (with a cute case) up to the mirror. She is looking at the phone screen in the reflection, not the camera.

Outfit (The "Lazy-Hot" Look):
*   Top: A tight, white ribbed tank top or baby tee, cropped short to show her midriff and belly button.
*   Bottom: Grey sweatpants worn low-rise (rolled down at the waist) to highlight her waist-to-hip ratio.

Environment: The room is a typical teenager's bedroom. Unmade bed in the background, soft LED strip lights (purple or warm white) on the ceiling, and maybe some clothes on a chair. It feels lived-in and authentic.

Lighting: Natural light coming from a window mixed with the soft indoor room light, creating a flattering, soft glow on her skin.
```

## High-Angle Birthday Dinner POV Photograph

> Fütüristik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
(Vertical 4:5 aspect ratio). A stunning, high-angle "POV" photograph taken at a trendy birthday dinner. The camera looks down slightly at the subject (friend's perspective).

Subject & Outfit: A beautiful young woman sits at the table, looking up directly at the camera with a radiant, magnetic smile and sparkling eyes.
*   Outfit: She wears a glamorous **{argument name="outfit type" default="White Satin Corset Top"}** or a **{argument name="outfit type 2" default="Silver Sequin Dress"}** with thin straps. The light-colored fabric illuminates her skin and adds a "celebratory goddess" vibe.
*   Hair & Makeup: Her hair is styled in voluminous blowout waves. Her makeup is "glowy and dewy", reflecting the warm light.

Action: She is caught in a candid moment of joy. One hand is playfully touching her hair or resting near her collarbone. In front of her on the table is the birthday cake with flickering candles.

Background (Depth): Behind her (seen from above) is the dark tropical plant wall with the neon sign "IT'S MY BIRTHDAY". Because of the high angle, the neon sign frames her head beautifully without being blocked.

Lighting (Golden Glow): The lighting is magical. The warm golden light from the cake candles illuminates her face from below, mixing with the soft ambient restaurant light. Her eyes catch the reflection of the candle flames (catchlights), making them look bright and alive. The background neon casts a soft purple/pink rim light on her hair.
```

## Upside-Down Flash Photo on Beach at Night

> Retro görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
(Vertical 4:5 aspect ratio). A creative, high-angle "upside-down" flash photograph taken on a sandy beach at night.

Composition (The Upside-Down Illusion): The image is framed "upside-down" from the viewer's perspective.
*   The Subject's Head is positioned at the BOTTOM of the frame.
*   Her Body extends upwards towards the TOP of the frame.
*   She is lounging on a vintage blue wooden beach chair.

Subject: A cool young woman with a short, ash-brown bob cut. Because her head is at the bottom, her hair fans out naturally onto the sand/chair (gravity effect). She wears "dewy" makeup with glossy pink lips that shine under the flash.
*   Tattoo: A delicate crescent moon tattoo with script is visible on her collarbone.
*   Pose: She lies flat on her back. One hand rests on her stomach; the other hand is raised near her face, fingers playfully touching her lips.

Outfit: She wears a white spaghetti-strap bikini top with a blue tropical palm tree print and light-wash denim shorts. On her feet (at the top of the frame) are light blue rubber flip-flops. She wears a gold sunburst pendant necklace (which falls towards her neck due to gravity).

Lighting & Setting: Direct, harsh "Flash Photography". The flash illuminates her brightly against the dark night, casting sharp, crisp shadows of the chair and her body onto the textured dry sand below. The chair has colorful rainbow-striped fabric, adding a pop of nostalgic color.
```

## Cinematic Wide-Angle of Monolith Shaped Like Persian Numeral Three

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A cinematic wide-angle photograph of a massive, ancient sandstone monolith naturally eroded into the shape of the Persian numeral three "۳". The monumental rock formation stands prominently in a vast desert landscape filled with endless rolling sand dunes. At its base, a small caravan of camels and people in traditional desert attire travels along a winding path, providing a sense of immense scale. The lighting is from a low-angled golden hour sun, casting long dramatic shadows and bathing the entire landscape in a warm, ethereal amber glow. Hyper-realistic details, intricate rock textures, clear sky, 8k resolution, National Geographic style.
```

## High-Contrast Black and White Tattooed Mirror Selfie

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A captivating young woman with platinum blonde hair takes a bold, intimate mirror selfie while seated on a modern metal chair in a minimalist, softly lit room. She wears a sleek black long-sleeve bodysuit made of smooth, slightly glossy fabric, featuring a daring open-front lace-up design with crisscross black ribbons that create a floral-shaped cutout over her cleavage, revealing tantalizing glimpses of skin. The bodysuit hugs her toned curves tightly, accentuating her hourglass figure and defined waist. Her legs are spread confidently wide apart, showcasing her full, heavily tattooed thighs and calves covered in an intricate sleeve of black-and-gray tattoos featuring mythical creatures, eagles, birds, feathers, and detailed organic designs that flow down to her bare feet. She holds her phone high with one hand, partially obscuring her face, while her other hand rests casually on her thigh. The black-and-white filter gives the image a dramatic, high-contrast, vintage film look with rich shadows and glowing highlights on her skin and fabric. The background is a clean, neutral studio space with a light gray floor and subtle wall texture. Ultra-realistic skin and tattoo details, strong cinematic lighting, moody and empowering atmosphere, highly detailed textures on lace-up ribbons, glossy fabric, and intricate ink work.
```

```
    "mirror": "mirror edges are cropped out of view"
  },
  "aesthetic": "high quality, photorealistic, lo-fi social media aesthetic",
  "makeup": {
    "blush": "prominent soft pink blush on her cheeks",
    "finish": "glowy makeup",
    "highlighter": "on the tip of her nose",
    "lips": "slightly parted soft warm pink lips"
  },
  "skin": "highly detailed skin texture",
  "lighting_detail": "even, invisible front illumination that softly brightens the face with neutral white light, no visible light source, no shadows, no reflections, no hotspots",
  "quality": "no blur"
}
```

## Cinematic Photojournalism of a Protest Subject

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.
